// Lock order: current_date -> current_sequence -> writer\n159\t        let mut current_date_guard = self\n160\t            .current_date\n161\t            .lock()\n162\t            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"failed to acquire current_date lock: {}\", e))?;\n163\t\n164\t        // Check if we need daily rotation\n165\t        let needs_daily_rotation = *current_date_guard != new_date;\n166\t\n167\t        if needs_daily_rotation {\n168\t            // New day, reset sequence number\n169\t            *current_date_guard = new_date.clone();\n170\t            let mut sequence_guard = self\n171\t                .current_sequence\n172\t                .lock()\n173\t                .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"failed to acquire current_sequence lock: {}\", e))?;\n174\t            *sequence_guard = 1;\n175\t            drop(sequence_guard);\n176\t        }\n177\t\n178\t        // Get next sequence number\n179\t        let mut sequence_guard = self\n180\t            .current_sequence\n181\t            .lock()\n182\t            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"failed to acquire current_sequence lock: {}\", e))?;\n183\t        let sequence = *sequence_guard;\n184\t        *sequence_guard += 1;\n185\t        drop(sequence_guard);\n186\t        drop(current_date_guard);\n187\t\n188\t        // Generate new filename\n189\t        let filename = Self::generate_filename(\n190\t            &self.worker_id,\n191\t            &self.session_id,\n192\t            new_date.as_str(),\n193\t            sequence,\n194\t        );\n195\t        let new_path = self.log_dir.join(&filename);\n196\t\n197\t        // Open new file\n198\t        let (new_file, size) = Self::open_file_with_size(&new_path)?;\n199\t        let new_writer = std::io::BufWriter::with_capacity(64 * 1024, new_file);\n200\t\n201\t        // Update writer and path\n202\t        let mut writer_guard = self\n203\t            .writer\n204\t            .lock()\n205\t            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"failed to acquire writer lock: {}\", e))?;\n206\t\n207\t        // Flush old writer before replacing\n208\t        let _ = writer_guard.flush();\n209\t\n210\t        // Replace writer\n211\t        *writer_guard = new_writer;\n212\t\n213\t        // Update size and path\n214\t        self.current_size\n215\t            .store(size, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);\n216\t        let mut path_guard = self\n217\t            .current_path\n218\t            .lock()\n219\t            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"failed to acquire current_path lock: {}\", e))?;\n220\t        *path_guard = new_path.clone();\n221\t        drop(path_guard);\n222\t\n223\t        Ok(new_path)\n224\t    }\n225\t\n226\t    /// Check if rotation is needed and rotate if so.\n227\t    ///\n228\t    /// Returns the current file path (may be newly rotated).\n229\t    fn ensure_rotation(&self) -> Result<PathBuf> {\n230\t        let now = Utc::now();\n231\t        let current_date = now.format(\"%Y-%m-%d\").to_string();\n232\t\n233\t        // Check if daily rotation is needed\n234\t        {\n235\t            let date_guard = self\n236\t                .current_date\n237\t                .lock()\n238\t                .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"failed to acquire current_date lock: {}\", e))?;\n239\t            if *date_guard != current_date {\n240\t                drop(date_guard);\n241\t                return self.rotate();\n242\t            }\n243\t        }\n244\t\n245\t        // Check if size-based rotation is needed\n246\t        let current_size = self.current_size.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);\n247\t        if current_size >= self.max_file_size {\n248\t            return self.rotate();\n249\t        }\n250\t\n251\t        // No rotation needed, return current path\n252\t        let path_guard = self\n253\t            .current_path\n254\t            .lock()\n255\t            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"failed to acquire current_path lock: {}\", e))?;\n256\t        Ok(path_guard.clone())\n257\t    }\n258\t\n259\t    /// Return the log directory.\n260\t    pub fn log_dir(&self) -> &Path {\n261\t        &self.log_dir\n262\t    }\n263\t\n264\t    /// Return the current log file path.\n265\t    ///\n266\t    /// This may change after rotation.\n267\t    pub fn path(&self) -> PathBuf {\n268\t        let guard = self.current_path.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());\n269\t        guard.clone()\n270\t    }\n271\t\n272\t    /// Get the current file size in bytes.\n273\t    pub fn current_file_size(&self) -> u64 {\n274\t        self.current_size.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed)\n275\t    }\n276\t\n277\t    /// Write a telemetry event to the file with automatic rotation.\n278\t    ///\n279\t    /// This method:\n280\t    /// 1. Checks if rotation is needed (daily or size-based)\n281\t    /// 2. Rotates to a new file if needed\n282\t    /// 3. Serializes the event to JSON\n283\t    /// 4. Writes the event to the current file\n284\t    /// 5. Updates the size counter\n285\t    ///\n286\t    /// # Errors\n287\t    ///\n288\t    /// Returns an error if:\n289\t    /// - File rotation fails\n290\t    /// - JSON serialization fails\n291\t    /// - File write fails\n292\t    /// - Locks are poisoned\n293\t    pub fn write_event(&self, event: &TelemetryEvent) -> Result<()> {\n294\t        // Check and perform rotation if needed\n295\t        self.ensure_rotation()\n296\t            .context(\"failed to check/perform rotation\")?;\n297\t\n298\t        // Serialize event to JSON\n299\t        let line = serde_json::to_string(event).context(\"failed to serialize event to JSON\")?;\n300\t\n301\t        let line_bytes = line.as_bytes();\n302\t        let line_len = line_bytes.len() as u64 + 1; // +1 for newline\n303\t\n304\t        // Write to file\n305\t        let mut writer = self\n306\t            .writer\n307\t            .lock()\n308\t            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"writer lock poisoned: {}\", e))?;\n309\t\n310\t        writeln!(writer, \"{}\", line)\n311\t            .with_context(|| \"failed to write event to log file\".to_string())?;\n312\t\n313\t        writer.flush().context(\"failed to flush log file\")?;\n314\t\n315\t        // Update size counter\n316\t        self.current_size\n317\t            .fetch_add(line_len, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);\n318\t\n319\t        Ok(())\n320\t    }\n321\t\n322\t    /// Write a boot event directly to the file, bypassing the normal channel.\n323\t    ///\n324\t    /// This is called immediately after FileSink creation to ensure that even if\n325\t    /// the writer thread fails to start, we have a trace in the JSONL file.\n326\t    /// The event is written synchronously and flushed to disk.\n327\t    pub fn write_boot_event_direct(\n328\t        &self,\n329\t        worker_id: &str,\n330\t        session_id: &str,\n331\t        version: &str,\n332\t    ) -> Result<()> {\n333\t        let current_path = self.path();\n334\t        Self::write_boot_event_direct_impl(\n335\t            &self.writer,\n336\t            &current_path,\n337\t            worker_id,\n338\t            session_id,\n339\t            version,\n340\t            time::Duration::from_secs(5), // 5 second timeout\n341\t        )\n342\t    }\n343\t\n344\t    /// Write a boot event directly to the file with a timeout.\n345\t    ///\n346\t    /// This is a timeout-aware variant that prevents indefinite blocking on hung\n347\t    /// filesystems (e.g., network filesystem issues, stale NFS mounts). If the\n348\t    /// write takes longer than the timeout, it returns an error and the caller\n349\t    /// can decide whether to continue or fail.\n350\t    ///\n351\t    /// The timeout is implemented by spawning a thread to do the blocking I/O\n352\t    /// and joining with a timeout. If the timeout expires, the thread is detached\n353\t    /// and will continue running (and eventually complete or be killed by the OS).\n354\t    fn write_boot_event_direct_impl(\n355\t        _writer: &Mutex<std::io::BufWriter<std::fs::File>>,\n356\t        path: &Path,\n357\t        worker_id: &str,\n358\t        session_id: &str,\n359\t        version: &str,\n360\t        timeout: time::Duration,\n361\t    ) -> Result<()> {\n362\t        use std::sync::mpsc;\n363\t        use std::thread;\n364\t\n365\t        let event = TelemetryEvent {\n366\t            timestamp: Utc::now(),\n367\t            event_type: \"worker.booting\".to_string(),\n368\t            worker_id: worker_id.to_string(),\n369\t            session_id: session_id.to_string(),\n370\t            sequence: 0,\n371\t            bead_id: None,\n372\t            workspace: None,\n373\t            duration_ms: None,\n374\t            data: serde_json::json!({ \"worker_name\": worker_id, \"version\": version }),\n375\t            trace_id: None,\n376\t            span_id: None,\n377\t        };\n378\t        let line = serde_json::to_string(&event)?;\n379\t        let path_for_error = path.display().to_string();\n380\t        let path_clone = path.to_path_buf();\n381\t\n382\t        // JoinHandle::join() has no timeout in Rust's std — use a channel with recv_timeout.\n383\t        let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel::<Result<(), String>>();\n384\t\n385\t        // The JoinHandle is intentionally dropped on timeout: dropping detaches the thread,\n386\t        // which continues running until it completes or the process exits.\n387\t        let _handle = thread::spawn(move || {\n388\t            let result: Result<(), String> = (|| {\n389\t                let file = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()\n390\t                    .create(true)\n391\t                    .append(true)\n392\t                    .open(&path_clone)\n393\t                    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;\n394\t                let mut writer = std::io::BufWriter::new(file);\n395\t                writeln!(writer, \"{line}\").map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;\n396\t                writer.flush().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;\n397\t                writer.get_ref().sync_all().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;\n398\t                Ok(())\n399\t            })();\n400\t            // Ignore send error: receiver may have timed out and been dropped.\n401\t            let _ = tx.send(result);\n402\t        });\n403\t\n404\t        match rx.recv_timeout(timeout) {\n405\t            Ok(Ok(())) => Ok(()),\n406\t            Ok(Err(e)) => Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\n407\t                \"failed to write boot event to {}: {}\",\n408\t                path_for_error,\n409\t                e\n410\t            )),\n411\t            Err(mpsc::RecvTimeoutError::Timeout) => Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\n412\t                \"timed out writing boot event to {} after {:?} (filesystem may be hung)\",\n413\t                path_for_error,\n414\t                timeout\n415\t            )),\n416\t            Err(mpsc::RecvTimeoutError::Disconnected) => Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\n417\t                \"boot event writer thread disconnected for {}\",\n418\t                path_for_error\n419\t            )),\n420\t        }\n421\t    }\n422\t}\n423\t\n424\timpl Sink for FileSink {\n425\t    fn accept(&self, event: &TelemetryEvent) -> Result<()> {\n426\t        // Use the new write_event method which handles rotation\n427\t        self.write_event(event)\n428\t    }\n429\t\n430\t    fn flush(&self, _deadline: time::Duration) -> Result<()> {\n431\t        let mut writer = self\n432\t            .writer\n433\t            .lock()\n434\t            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"writer lock poisoned: {}\", e))?;\n435\t        writer.flush()?;\n436\t        // fsync to ensure durability before shutdown\n437\t        writer.get_ref().sync_all()?;\n438\t        Ok(())\n439\t    }\n440\t}\n441\t\n442\t#[cfg(test)]\n443\tmod tests {\n444\t    use super::*;\n445\t    use std::ffi::OsString;\n446\t    use std::fs;\n447\t    use std::sync::{Mutex, OnceLock};\n448\t    use tempfile::TempDir;\n449\t\n450\t    struct HomeGuard {\n451\t        previous: Option<OsString>,\n452\t    }\n453\t\n454\t    impl HomeGuard {\n455\t        fn set(path: &Path) -> Self {\n456\t            let previous = std::env::var_os(\"HOME\");\n457\t            std::env::set_var(\"HOME\", path);\n458\t            Self { previous }\n459\t        }\n460\t    }\n461\t\n462\t    impl Drop for File sink for JSONL telemetry.\n//!\n//! Writes structured events to `<log_dir>/<worker>-<session>-<date>.jsonl`.\n//! Supports daily and size-based file rotation.\n//!\n//! ## Rotation\n//!\n//! - **Daily rotation**: Creates a new file at midnight (UTC)\n//! - **Size-based rotation**: Creates a new file when size limit is reached\n//! - File naming: `{worker_id}-{session_id}-{date}.jsonl`\n//!   - Date format: `YYYY-MM-DD`\n//!   - Multiple files per day are numbered: `...-001.jsonl`, `...-002.jsonl`\n\nuse anyhow::{Context, Result};\nuse chrono::Utc;\nuse std::fs::OpenOptions;\nuse std::io::Write;\nuse std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\nuse std::sync::{atomic::AtomicU64, Mutex};\nuse std::time;\n\nuse super::{Sink, TelemetryEvent};\n\n/// Maximum size of a single log file before rotation (default: 100 MB)\nconst DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES: u64 = 100 * 1024 * 1024;\n\n/// Writes JSONL telemetry to `<log_dir>/<worker>-<session>-<date>.jsonl`.\n///\n/// Supports both daily and size-based file rotation. Events are written\n/// atomically with proper error handling and file management.\npub struct FileSink {\n    /// Log directory where events are written.\n    log_dir: PathBuf,\n    /// Worker identifier for file naming.\n    worker_id: String,\n    /// Session identifier for file naming.\n    session_id: String,\n    /// Current file path (for rotation tracking).\n    current_path: Mutex<PathBuf>,\n    /// Current file date (for daily rotation tracking).\n    current_date: Mutex<String>,\n    /// Current file sequence number (for multiple files per day).\n    current_sequence: Mutex<u32>,\n    /// Maximum file size before rotation (bytes).\n    max_file_size: u64,\n    /// Current file size estimate (atomic for non-blocking reads).\n    current_size: AtomicU64,\n    /// Buffered writer (replaced on rotation).\n    writer: Mutex<std::io::BufWriter<std::fs::File>>,\n}\n\nimpl FileSink {\n    /// Construct a sink using the default log directory (`~/.needle/logs/`).\n    ///\n    /// Uses default rotation settings (100 MB max file size, daily rotation).\n    pub fn new(worker_id: &str, session_id: &str) -> Result<Self> {\n        let home = std::env::var(\"HOME\").unwrap_or_else(|_| \".\".to_string());\n        let log_dir = PathBuf::from(&home).join(\".needle\").join(\"logs\");\n        Self::with_config(log_dir, worker_id, session_id, DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES)\n    }\n\n    /// Construct a sink writing to a specific directory with custom size limit.\n    ///\n    /// Creates the directory (and parents) if it does not exist.\n    ///\n    /// # Arguments\n    /// * `log_dir` - Directory where log files will be written\n    /// * `worker_id` - Worker identifier\n    /// * `session_id` - Session identifier (8 hex chars)\n    /// * `max_file_size_bytes` - Maximum size of each log file before rotation\n    pub fn with_config(\n        log_dir: PathBuf,\n        worker_id: &str,\n        session_id: &str,\n        max_file_size_bytes: u64,\n    ) -> Result<Self> {\n        // Validate/log_dir exists or can be created\n        if !log_dir.exists() {\n            std::fs::create_dir_all(&log_dir).with_context(|| {\n                format!(\"failed to create log directory: {}\", log_dir.display())\n            })?;\n        }\n\n        let current_date = Utc::now().format(\"%Y-%m-%d\").to_string();\n        let filename = format!(\"{}-{}-{}.jsonl\", worker_id, session_id, current_date);\n        let path = log_dir.join(&filename);\n\n        let (file, size) = Self::open_file_with_size(&path)?;\n        let writer = std::io::BufWriter::with_capacity(64 * 1024, file);\n\n        Ok(FileSink {\n            log_dir,\n            worker_id: worker_id.to_string(),\n            session_id: session_id.to_string(),\n            current_path: Mutex::new(path),\n            current_date: Mutex::new(current_date),\n            current_sequence: Mutex::new(1),\n            max_file_size: max_file_size_bytes,\n            current_size: AtomicU64::new(size),\n            writer: Mutex::new(writer),\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Construct a sink writing to a specific directory (legacy compatibility).\n    ///\n    /// Creates the directory (and parents) if it does not exist.\n    /// Uses default rotation settings.\n    ///\n    /// # Arguments\n    /// * `log_dir` - Directory where log files will be written\n    /// * `worker_id` - Worker identifier\n    /// * `session_id` - Session identifier (8 hex chars)\n    pub fn with_dir(log_dir: PathBuf, worker_id: &str, session_id: &str) -> Result<Self> {\n        Self::with_config(log_dir, worker_id, session_id, DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES)\n    }\n\n    /// Open a file and return it with its current size.\n    ///\n    /// If the file exists, appends to it. If not, creates a new file.\n    fn open_file_with_size(path: &Path) -> Result<(std::fs::File, u64)> {\n        let mut options = OpenOptions::new();\n        options.create(true).append(true);\n        // Telemetry lines can carry sanitized transcript content, so restrict\n        // newly created log files to the owner. This applies at creation only;\n        // an existing file keeps its current mode.\n        #[cfg(unix)]\n        {\n            use std::os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt;\n            options.mode(0o600);\n        }\n        let file = options\n            .open(path)\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to open log file: {}\", path.display()))?;\n\n        let size = file\n            .metadata()\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to get metadata for: {}\", path.display()))?\n            .len();\n\n        Ok((file, size))\n    }\n\n    /// Generate a new filename with date and sequence number.\n    fn generate_filename(worker_id: &str, session_id: &str, date: &str, sequence: u32) -> String {\n        format!(\n            \"{}-{}-{}-{:03}.jsonl\",\n            worker_id, session_id, date, sequence\n        )\n    }\n\n    /// Rotate the log file (daily or size-based).\n    ///\n    /// Creates a new log file and updates the writer. Returns the new file path.\n    fn rotate(&self) -> Result<PathBuf> {\n        let now = Utc::now();\n        let new_date = now.format(\"%Y-%m-%d\").to_string();\n\n        // Lock order: current_date -> current_sequence -> writer\n        let mut current_date_guard = self\n            .current_date\n            .lock()\n            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"failed to acquire current_date lock: {}\", e))?;\n\n        // Check if we need daily rotation\n        let needs_daily_rotation = *current_date_guard != new_date;\n\n        if needs_daily_rotation {\n            // New day, reset sequence number\n            *current_date_guard = new_date.clone();\n            let mut sequence_guard = self\n                .current_sequence\n                .lock()\n                .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"failed to acquire current_sequence lock: {}\", e))?;\n            *sequence_guard = 1;\n            drop(sequence_guard);\n        }\n\n        // Get next sequence number\n        let mut sequence_guard = self\n            .current_sequence\n            .lock()\n            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"failed to acquire current_sequence lock: {}\", e))?;\n        let sequence = *sequence_guard;\n        *sequence_guard += 1;\n        drop(sequence_guard);\n        drop(current_date_guard);\n\n        // Generate new filename\n        let filename = Self::generate_filename(\n            &self.worker_id,\n            &self.session_id,\n            new_date.as_str(),\n            sequence,\n        );\n        let new_path = self.log_dir.join(&filename);\n\n        // Open new file\n        let (new_file, size) = Self::open_file_with_size(&new_path)?;\n        let new_writer = std::io::BufWriter::with_capacity(64 * 1024, new_file);\n\n        // Update writer and path\n        let mut writer_guard = self\n            .writer\n            .lock()\n            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"failed to acquire writer lock: {}\", e))?;\n\n        // Flush old writer before replacing\n        let _ = writer_guard.flush();\n\n        // Replace writer\n        *writer_guard = new_writer;\n\n        // Update size and path\n        self.current_size\n            .store(size, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);\n        let mut path_guard = self\n            .current_path\n            .lock()\n            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"failed to acquire current_path lock: {}\", e))?;\n        *path_guard = new_path.clone();\n        drop(path_guard);\n\n        Ok(new_path)\n    }\n\n    /// Check if rotation is needed and rotate if so.\n    ///\n    /// Returns the current file path (may be newly rotated).\n    fn ensure_rotation(&self) -> Result<PathBuf> {\n        let now = Utc::now();\n        let current_date = now.format(\"%Y-%m-%d\").to_string();\n\n        // Check if daily rotation is needed\n        {\n            let date_guard = self\n                .current_date\n                .lock()\n                .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"failed to acquire current_date lock: {}\", e))?;\n            if *date_guard != current_date {\n                drop(date_guard);\n                return self.rotate();\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Check if size-based rotation is needed\n        let current_size = self.current_size.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);\n        if current_size >= self.max_file_size {\n            return self.rotate();\n        }\n\n        // No rotation needed, return current path\n        let path_guard = self\n            .current_path\n            .lock()\n            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"failed to acquire current_path lock: {}\", e))?;\n        Ok(path_guard.clone())\n    }\n\n    /// Return the log directory.\n    pub fn log_dir(&self) -> &Path {\n        &self.log_dir\n    }\n\n    /// Return the current log file path.\n    ///\n    /// This may change after rotation.\n    pub fn path(&self) -> PathBuf {\n        let guard = self.current_path.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());\n        guard.clone()\n    }\n\n    /// Get the current file size in bytes.\n    pub fn current_file_size(&self) -> u64 {\n        self.current_size.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed)\n    }\n\n    /// Write a telemetry event to the file with automatic rotation.\n    ///\n    /// This method:\n    /// 1. Checks if rotation is needed (daily or size-based)\n    /// 2. Rotates to a new file if needed\n    /// 3. Serializes the event to JSON\n    /// 4. Writes the event to the current file\n    /// 5. Updates the size counter\n    ///\n    /// # Errors\n    ///\n    /// Returns an error if:\n    /// - File rotation fails\n    /// - JSON serialization fails\n    /// - File write fails\n    /// - Locks are poisoned\n    pub fn write_event(&self, event: &TelemetryEvent) -> Result<()> {\n        // Check and perform rotation if needed\n        self.ensure_rotation()\n            .context(\"failed to check/perform rotation\")?;\n\n        // Serialize event to JSON\n        let line = serde_json::to_string(event).context(\"failed to serialize event to JSON\")?;\n\n        let line_bytes = line.as_bytes();\n        let line_len = line_bytes.len() as u64 + 1; // +1 for newline\n\n        // Write to file\n        let mut writer = self\n            .writer\n            .lock()\n            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"writer lock poisoned: {}\", e))?;\n\n        writeln!(writer, \"{}\", line)\n            .with_context(|| \"failed to write event to log file\".to_string())?;\n\n        writer.flush().context(\"failed to flush log file\")?;\n\n        // Update size counter\n        self.current_size\n            .fetch_add(line_len, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Write a boot event directly to the file, bypassing the normal channel.\n    ///\n    /// This is called immediately after FileSink creation to ensure that even if\n    /// the writer thread fails to start, we have a trace in the JSONL file.\n    /// The event is written synchronously and flushed to disk.\n    pub fn write_boot_event_direct(\n        &self,\n        worker_id: &str,\n        session_id: &str,\n        version: &str,\n    ) -> Result<()> {\n        let current_path = self.path();\n        Self::write_boot_event_direct_impl(\n            &self.writer,\n            &current_path,\n            worker_id,\n            session_id,\n            version,\n            time::Duration::from_secs(5), // 5 second timeout\n        )\n    }\n\n    /// Write a boot event directly to the file with a timeout.\n    ///\n    /// This is a timeout-aware variant that prevents indefinite blocking on hung\n    /// filesystems (e.g., network filesystem issues, stale NFS mounts). If the\n    /// write takes longer than the timeout, it returns an error and the caller\n    /// can decide whether to continue or fail.\n    ///\n    /// The timeout is implemented by spawning a thread to do the blocking I/O\n    /// and joining with a timeout. If the timeout expires, the thread is detached\n    /// and will continue running (and eventually complete or be killed by the OS).\n    fn write_boot_event_direct_impl(\n        _writer: &Mutex<std::io::BufWriter<std::fs::File>>,\n        path: &Path,\n        worker_id: &str,\n        session_id: &str,\n        version: &str,\n        timeout: time::Duration,\n    ) -> Result<()> {\n        use std::sync::mpsc;\n        use std::thread;\n\n        let event = TelemetryEvent {\n            timestamp: Utc::now(),\n            event_type: \"worker.booting\".to_string(),\n            worker_id: worker_id.to_string(),\n            session_id: session_id.to_string(),\n            sequence: 0,\n            bead_id: None,\n            workspace: None,\n            duration_ms: None,\n            data: serde_json::json!({ \"worker_name\": worker_id, \"version\": version }),\n            trace_id: None,\n            span_id: None,\n        };\n        let line = serde_json::to_string(&event)?;\n        let path_for_error = path.display().to_string();\n        let path_clone = path.to_path_buf();\n\n        // JoinHandle::join() has no timeout in Rust's std — use a channel with recv_timeout.\n        let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel::<Result<(), String>>();\n\n        // The JoinHandle is intentionally dropped on timeout: dropping detaches the thread,\n        // which continues running until it completes or the process exits.\n        let _handle = thread::spawn(move || {\n            let result: Result<(), String> = (|| {\n                let file = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()\n                    .create(true)\n                    .append(true)\n                    .open(&path_clone)\n                    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;\n                let mut writer = std::io::BufWriter::new(file);\n                writeln!(writer, \"{line}\").map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;\n                writer.flush().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;\n                writer.get_ref().sync_all().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;\n                Ok(())\n            })();\n            // Ignore send error: receiver may have timed out and been dropped.\n            let _ = tx.send(result);\n        });\n\n        match rx.recv_timeout(timeout) {\n            Ok(Ok(())) => Ok(()),\n            Ok(Err(e)) => Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\n                \"failed to write boot event to {}: {}\",\n                path_for_error,\n                e\n            )),\n            Err(mpsc::RecvTimeoutError::Timeout) => Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\n                \"timed out writing boot event to {} after {:?} (filesystem may be hung)\",\n                path_for_error,\n                timeout\n            )),\n            Err(mpsc::RecvTimeoutError::Disconnected) => Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\n                \"boot event writer thread disconnected for {}\",\n                path_for_error\n            )),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Sink for FileSink {\n    fn accept(&self, event: &TelemetryEvent) -> Result<()> {\n        // Use the new write_event method which handles rotation\n        self.write_event(event)\n    }\n\n    fn flush(&self, _deadline: time::Duration) -> Result<()> {\n        let mut writer = self\n            .writer\n            .lock()\n            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"writer lock poisoned: {}\", e))?;\n        writer.flush()?;\n        // fsync to ensure durability before shutdown\n        writer.get_ref().sync_all()?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use std::ffi::OsString;\n    use std::fs;\n    use std::sync::{Mutex, OnceLock};\n    use tempfile::TempDir;\n\n    struct HomeGuard {\n        previous: Option<OsString>,\n    }\n\n    impl HomeGuard {\n        fn set(path: &Path) -> Self {\n            let previous = std::env::var_os(\"HOME\");\n            std::env::set_var(\"HOME\", path);\n            Self { previous }\n        }\n    }\n\n    impl Drop for HomeGuard {\n        fn drop(&mut self) {\n            match self.previous.take() {\n                Some(previous) => std::env::set_var(\"HOME\", previous),\n                None => std::env::remove_var(\"HOME\"),\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn home_lock() -> std::sync::MutexGuard<'static, ()> {\n        static HOME_LOCK: OnceLock<Mutex<()>> = OnceLock::new();\n
